<div class='question question'>

Give all numbers which are factors of both $32$ and $24$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Factors of 32}  &=1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32 \\\\
\text{Factors of 24}  &=1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12, 24 \\\\
\text{Common factors} &=1, 2, 4, 8
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1, 2, 4, 8$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Factors of 32}  &=1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32 \\\\
\text{Factors of 24}  &=1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12, 24 \\\\
\text{Common factors} &=1, 2, 4, 8
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1, 2, 4, 8$
</code></pre>
</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

Write down a number between $0.3$ and $\dfrac{2}{5}$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (0.3 + \dfrac {2} {5}) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (0.3 + 0.4) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 0.7 \\\\
&= \dfrac {0.7} {2} \\\\
&= 0.35
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.35$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (0.3 + \dfrac {2} {5}) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (0.3 + 0.4) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 0.7 \\\\
&= \dfrac {0.7} {2} \\\\
&= 0.35
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$0.35$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question question'>


$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
         &            &   2   &   *   &   8 \\
         &   +        &   2   &   9   &   9 \\
\hline 
         &            &   5   &   7   &   7 
\end{array}
$

Which of the following gives the correct number for $*$ ?

- $3$

- $4$

- $5$

- $6$

- $7$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
    &  5     &   \boxed {\green {7}}   &   7 \\
{}- &  2     &   9   &   9 \\
\hline
    &  2     &   7   &   8
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>E</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

7

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
    &  5     &   \boxed {\rtq_katex_color_answer {7}}   &   7 \\
{}- &  2     &   9   &   9 \\
\hline
    &  2     &   7   &   8
\end{array}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>E</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>7
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

I have lots of $5\text{p}$, $8\text{p}$ and $11\text{p}$ stamps. What’s the biggest amount I cannot make exactly with my stamps?

- $7\text{p}$

- $49\text{p}$

- $19\text{p}$

- $38\text{p}$

- $17\text{p}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$17\text{p}$

Lets verify

$
\begin{aligned}
7\text{p}  &= 5 \text{p} \times 2 + 8\text{p} -11\text{p}\\\\
49\text{p}   &= 11 \text{p} \times 4 + 5\text{p} \\\\
19\text{p}   &= 11 \text{p} + 8 \text{p} \\\\
38\text{p}   &= 11 \text{p} \times 3 + 5 \text{p} 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>E</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

 $=$ $17\text{p}$


</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$17\text{p}$
<emptyline>
Lets verify
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
7\text{p}  &= 5 \text{p} \times 2 + 8\text{p} -11\text{p}\\\\
49\text{p}   &= 11 \text{p} \times 4 + 5\text{p} \\\\
19\text{p}   &= 11 \text{p} + 8 \text{p} \\\\
38\text{p}   &= 11 \text{p} \times 3 + 5 \text{p} 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>E</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'></code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$17\text{p}$

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='workings'>

$
\begin{array}
&          &  3  &  7  &  5 \\
& {}\times &     &  2  &  3 \\

&     7    &  5  &  0  &  0 \\

$

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Amran wrote down the calculation:

$\dfrac{6000 \times 20} {30} = 40$

Her teacher looked at the answer and said it could not be correct.

Explain how her teacher knew the answer must be wrong.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$\dfrac{6000 \times 20} {30} = 40$

Lets verify.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{6000 \times 20} {30} \\\\ 
&= \dfrac{30 \times 200 \times 20} {30} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel{30} \times 200 \times 20} {\cancel{30}} \\\\
&= 200 \times 20 \\\\
&= 4000
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$4000$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac{6000 \times 20} {30} = 40$
<emptyline>
Lets verify.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{6000 \times 20} {30} \\\\ 
&= \dfrac{30 \times 200 \times 20} {30} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel{30} \times 200 \times 20} {\cancel{30}} \\\\
&= 200 \times 20 \\\\
&= 4000
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$600\div24 = 25$ 

Fill in the gap

$600 \div \ldots = 50$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the missing number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
600 \div a              &= 50 \\\\
\dfrac {600} {a}        &= 50 \\\\
600                     &= 50 \times a \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {600} {50}  \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {600} {25 \times 2}  \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {24} {2}  \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {12 \times 2} {2}  \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}}  \\\\
a                       &= 12
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$12$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the missing number $= a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
600 \div a              &= 50 \\\\
\dfrac {600} {a}        &= 50 \\\\
600                     &= 50 \times a \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {600} {50}  \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {600} {25 \times 2}  \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {24} {2}  \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {12 \times 2} {2}  \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}}  \\\\
a                       &= 12
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$12$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

<div class='question subquestion'>

$20\%$ of $\pounds 840$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 20 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 840 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {100} \times 840 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 840} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \cancel{0} \times 84 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= 2 \times 84  \\\\
&= \pounds 168
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

$12 \dfrac{1}{2}\%$ of $6.80 \ \text{metres}$, giving your answer in **centimetres**.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 12 \ \dfrac {1} {2} \% \ \text {of} \ 6.80 \ \text{meters} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {2 \times 100} \times 6.8 \times 100 \ \text{centimeters} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {2 \times 100} \times {680}  \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 680} {2 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25 \times 2 \times 4 \times 85} {2 \times 4 \times 25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel{25} \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{4} \times 85} {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{4} \times \cancel{25}} \\\\
&= 85 \ \text{centimeters}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class='answer'>

$85 \ \text{centimeters}$

$

$2 \dfrac{3}{5} + 1 \dfrac{1}{2}$

<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2 \dfrac {3} {5} + 1 \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13} {5} + \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13 \times 2} {5 \times 2} + \dfrac {3 \times 5} {2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {26} {10} + \dfrac {15} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {26 + 15} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {41} {10} \\\\
&= 4 \dfrac {1} {10} 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$4 \dfrac {1} {10}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>

\begin{aligned}
&= 2 \dfrac {3} {5} + 1 \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13} {5} + \dfrac {3} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13 \times 2} {5 \times 2} + \dfrac {3 \times 5} {2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {26} {10} + \dfrac {15} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {26 + 15} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {41} {10} \\\\
&= 4 \dfrac {1} {10} 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question subquestion'>

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$4 \ \boxed{\green{\times}} \ 3\  \boxed{\green{\times}} \ 2 = 14$

Let's verify.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \times 3  + 2 \\\\
&= 12 + 2 \\\\
&= 14
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$4 \times 3 + 2 = 14$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$4 \ \boxed{\rtq_katex_color_answer{\times}} \ 3\  \boxed{\rtq_katex_color_answer{\times}} \ 2 = 14$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$(3 \ \boxed{\green{+}} \ 5) \ \boxed{\green{\div}} \ (4 \ \boxed{\green{-}} \  2) = 4$

Let's verify.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= (3 + 5) \div (4 - 2) \\\\
&= 8 \div 2 \\\\
&= 4
\end{aligned}
$
